    DROP INDEX [IX_Users_PhoneNumber] ON [dbo].[Users];

SELECT TOP(@p) [c].[CourseId], [c].[Title], [c].[Description], [c].[Price], COALESCE([c].[DurationHours], 0) AS [DurationHours], [c].[ImageName], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [CourseVideos] AS [c0]
    WHERE [c].[CourseId] = [c0].[CourseId] AND [c0].[IsActive] = CAST(1 AS bit)) AS [VideoCount], [c].[IsBundle], [c].[DiscountPrice], [c].[IsDiscountActive], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [CourseBundleItems] AS [c1]
    WHERE [c].[CourseId] = [c1].[BundleId]) AS [SubCourseCount]
FROM [Courses] AS [c]
WHERE [c].[IsActive] = CAST(1 AS bit) AND [c].[IsListedForSale] = CAST(1 AS bit)
ORDER BY [c].[CreateDate] DESC

SELECT CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [UserRoles] AS [u]
        INNER JOIN [Roles] AS [r] ON [u].[RoleId] = [r].[RoleId]
        WHERE [u].[UserId] = @userId AND [r].[IsActive] = CAST(1 AS bit) AND EXISTS (
            SELECT 1
            FROM [RolePermissions] AS [r0]
            INNER JOIN [Permissions] AS [p] ON [r0].[PermissionId] = [p].[PermissionId]
            WHERE [r].[RoleId] = [r0].[RoleId] AND [p].[PermissionName] = @permissionName)) THEN CAST(1 AS bit)
